## 📄 File: `backend\app\api.py`

```python
from typing import Annotated

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.attempts import (
    create_attempt,
    finish_attempt,
    get_attempt_state,
    list_attempts,
    list_modules,
    record_attention_action,
    record_correction_action,
    record_memory_answer,
    record_recall,
)
from app.database import get_session
from app.schemas import (
    AttemptHistoryItem,
    AttemptState,
    AttentionActionRequest,
    CorrectionActionRequest,
    HealthResponse,
    MemoryAnswerRequest,
    ModuleSummary,
    RecallRequest,
    StartAttemptRequest,
)

router = APIRouter()
SessionDep = Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_session)]


@router.get("/health", response_model=HealthResponse, tags=["system"])
async def health(session: SessionDep) -> HealthResponse:
    await session.execute(text("SELECT 1"))
    return HealthResponse(status="ok")


@router.get("/modules", response_model=list[ModuleSummary], tags=["modules"])
async def modules(session: SessionDep) -> list[ModuleSummary]:
    return await list_modules(session)


@router.post("/attempts", response_model=AttemptState, tags=["attempts"])
async def start_attempt(request: StartAttemptRequest, session: SessionDep) -> AttemptState:
    return await create_attempt(session, request.module_code)


@router.get("/attempts", response_model=list[AttemptHistoryItem], tags=["attempts"])
async def attempts(session: SessionDep) -> list[AttemptHistoryItem]:
    return await list_attempts(session)


@router.get("/attempts/{attempt_id}", response_model=AttemptState, tags=["attempts"])
async def attempt_state(attempt_id: int, session: SessionDep) -> AttemptState:
    return await get_attempt_state(session, attempt_id)


@router.post("/attempts/{attempt_id}/actions", response_model=AttemptState, tags=["attempts"])
async def attention_action(
    attempt_id: int,
    request: AttentionActionRequest,
    session: SessionDep,
) -> AttemptState:
    return await record_attention_action(session, attempt_id, request)


@router.post("/attempts/{attempt_id}/finish", response_model=AttemptState, tags=["attempts"])
async def finish(attempt_id: int, session: SessionDep) -> AttemptState:
    return await finish_attempt(session, attempt_id)


@router.post(
    "/attempts/{attempt_id}/rounds/{round_number}/answer",
    response_model=AttemptState,
    tags=["attempts"],
)
async def memory_answer(
    attempt_id: int,
    round_number: int,
    request: MemoryAnswerRequest,
    session: SessionDep,
) -> AttemptState:
    return await record_memory_answer(session, attempt_id, round_number, request)


@router.post(
    "/attempts/{attempt_id}/correction/actions", response_model=AttemptState, tags=["attempts"]
)
async def correction_action(
    attempt_id: int,
    request: CorrectionActionRequest,
    session: SessionDep,
) -> AttemptState:
    return await record_correction_action(session, attempt_id, request)


@router.post("/attempts/{attempt_id}/recall", response_model=AttemptState, tags=["attempts"])
async def recall(attempt_id: int, request: RecallRequest, session: SessionDep) -> AttemptState:
    return await record_recall(session, attempt_id, request)
```

## 📄 File: `backend\app\attempts.py`

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
from decimal import Decimal
from difflib import SequenceMatcher

from fastapi import HTTPException, status
from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy.orm import joinedload, selectinload

from app.config import get_settings
from app.generators import (
    generate_correction_grid,
    generate_memory_rounds,
    generate_number_grid,
    generate_statements,
)
from app.models import (
    Attempt,
    AttemptEvent,
    AttemptRound,
    AttemptStatus,
    EventType,
    FinishReason,
    ModuleLevel,
    OperatorStats,
    TrainingModule,
    User,
)
from app.schemas import (
    AttemptHistoryItem,
    AttemptMetrics,
    AttemptState,
    AttentionActionRequest,
    CorrectionActionRequest,
    MemoryAnswerRequest,
    MemoryRoundState,
    ModuleSummary,
    RecallRequest,
)
from app.scoring import attention_grade, evaluate_memory_answer, per_minute_pace


def utc_now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(UTC)


def elapsed_ms(attempt: Attempt, now: datetime | None = None) -> int:
    current = now or utc_now()
    started_at = attempt.started_at
    if started_at.tzinfo is None:
        current = current.replace(tzinfo=None)
    return max(0, int((current - started_at).total_seconds() * 1000))


def as_float(value: Decimal | None) -> float | None:
    return float(value) if value is not None else None


def as_utc(value: datetime | None) -> datetime | None:
    if value is None or value.tzinfo is not None:
        return value
    return value.replace(tzinfo=UTC)


def next_event_sequence(attempt: Attempt) -> int:
    return max((event.sequence_number for event in attempt.events), default=-1) + 1


async def get_demo_operator(session: AsyncSession) -> User:
    operator = await session.scalar(
        select(User).where(User.email == get_settings().demo_operator_email)
    )
    if operator is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            detail="Демонстрационный оператор не создан. Выполните начальное заполнение БД.",
        )
    return operator


def attempt_query(attempt_id: int, operator_id: int, *, lock: bool = False):
    query = (
        select(Attempt)
        .where(Attempt.id == attempt_id, Attempt.operator_id == operator_id)
        .options(
            joinedload(Attempt.level).joinedload(ModuleLevel.module),
            selectinload(Attempt.events),
            selectinload(Attempt.rounds),
        )
    )
    return query.with_for_update(of=Attempt) if lock else query


async def load_attempt(
    session: AsyncSession, attempt_id: int, operator_id: int, *, lock: bool = False
) -> Attempt:
    attempt = await session.scalar(attempt_query(attempt_id, operator_id, lock=lock))
    if attempt is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Попытка не найдена")
    return attempt


def build_attempt_state(attempt: Attempt) -> AttemptState:
    module = attempt.level.module
    config = attempt.level_config_snapshot
    started_at = as_utc(attempt.started_at)
    assert started_at is not None
    deadline = None
    current_round = None

    if module.exercise_type == "sequential_search":
        deadline = started_at + timedelta(milliseconds=config["time_limit_ms"])
        task = {
            **attempt.task_snapshot,
            "grid_rows": config["grid_rows"],
            "grid_columns": config["grid_columns"],
            "min_number": config["min_number"],
            "max_number": config["max_number"],
            "time_limit_ms": config["time_limit_ms"],
            "font_size_pt": config.get("font_size_pt", 16),
            "font_style": config.get("font_style", "standard"),
            "show_cell_borders": config.get("show_cell_borders", True),
            "next_number": min(
                config["min_number"] + attempt.items_found,
                config["max_number"],
            ),
            "next_action_sequence": next_event_sequence(attempt),
            "normative_range": config["normative_range"],
        }
    elif module.exercise_type in {"correction", "dual_correction"}:
        deadline = started_at + timedelta(milliseconds=config["time_limit_ms"])
        scan_elapsed = min(elapsed_ms(attempt), config["time_limit_ms"])
        stage = (
            "recall"
            if module.exercise_type == "dual_correction" and scan_elapsed >= config["time_limit_ms"]
            else "scan"
        )
        selected_positions = [
            event.grid_position
            for event in attempt.events
            if event.event_type == EventType.correct_click and event.event_data.get("selected")
        ]
        wrong_positions = [
            event.grid_position
            for event in attempt.events
            if event.event_type == EventType.wrong_click and event.event_data.get("selected")
        ]
        task = {
            "cells": attempt.task_snapshot["cells"],
            "grid_rows": config["grid_rows"],
            "grid_columns": config["grid_columns"],
            "target_symbol": config["target_symbol"],
            "time_limit_ms": config["time_limit_ms"],
            "line_interval_ms": config["line_interval_ms"],
            "cursor": attempt.last_found_number if attempt.last_found_number is not None else -1,
            "selected_positions": selected_positions,
            "wrong_positions": wrong_positions,
            "line_marks": correction_line_marks(attempt, scan_elapsed),
            "next_action_sequence": next_event_sequence(attempt),
            "stage": stage,
        }
        if module.exercise_type == "dual_correction" and stage == "scan":
            interval = config["statement_interval_ms"]
            index = scan_elapsed // interval
            within = scan_elapsed % interval
            if index < config["statement_count"] and within < config["statement_visible_ms"]:
                task["visible_statement"] = attempt.task_snapshot["statements"][index]
                task["visible_statement_number"] = index + 1
    else:
        task = {
            "grid_rows": config["grid_rows"],
            "grid_columns": config["grid_columns"],
            "round_count": config["round_count"],
            "shape_count": config["shape_count"],
            "shapes": config["shapes"],
        }
        active_round = next((item for item in attempt.rounds if item.answered_at is None), None)
        if attempt.status == AttemptStatus.in_progress and active_round is not None:
            current_round = MemoryRoundState(
                round_number=active_round.round_number,
                total_rounds=config["round_count"],
                exposure_ms=config["exposure_ms"],
                stimulus=active_round.stimulus,
            )

    return AttemptState(
        id=attempt.id,
        module_code=module.code,
        module_title=module.title,
        status=attempt.status,
        finish_reason=attempt.finish_reason,
        started_at=started_at,
        deadline_at=deadline,
        duration_ms=attempt.duration_ms,
        task=task,
        metrics=AttemptMetrics(
            score=as_float(attempt.score),
            accuracy=as_float(attempt.accuracy),
            correct_actions=attempt.correct_actions,
            incorrect_actions=attempt.incorrect_actions,
            total_actions=attempt.total_actions,
            items_found=attempt.items_found,
            average_reaction_ms=attempt.average_reaction_ms,
            max_reaction_ms=attempt.max_reaction_ms,
            speed_per_minute=as_float(attempt.speed_per_minute),
        ),
        current_round=current_round,
        result=attempt.result_data,
    )


async def create_attempt(session: AsyncSession, module_code: str) -> AttemptState:
    operator = await get_demo_operator(session)
    level = await session.scalar(
        select(ModuleLevel)
        .join(ModuleLevel.module)
        .where(
            TrainingModule.code == module_code,
            TrainingModule.is_active.is_(True),
            ModuleLevel.level_number == 1,
            ModuleLevel.is_active.is_(True),
        )
        .options(joinedload(ModuleLevel.module))
    )
    if level is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Модуль не найден")

    active = await session.scalar(
        select(Attempt)
        .where(
            Attempt.operator_id == operator.id,
            Attempt.level_id == level.id,
            Attempt.status == AttemptStatus.in_progress,
        )
        .order_by(Attempt.started_at.desc())
        .options(
            joinedload(Attempt.level).joinedload(ModuleLevel.module),
            selectinload(Attempt.events),
            selectinload(Attempt.rounds),
        )
        .limit(1)
    )
    if active is not None:
        await ensure_attention_timeout(session, active)
        if active.status == AttemptStatus.in_progress:
            await session.commit()
            return build_attempt_state(active)

    config = dict(level.config)
    if level.module.exercise_type == "sequential_search":
        number_count = config["max_number"] - config["min_number"] + 1
        task_snapshot = {"cells": generate_number_grid(number_count, start=config["min_number"])}
    elif level.module.exercise_type in {"correction", "dual_correction"}:
        task_snapshot = {
            "cells": generate_correction_grid(
                config["grid_rows"],
                config["grid_columns"],
                config["symbols"],
                config["target_symbol"],
                config["target_share"],
            )
        }
        if level.module.exercise_type == "dual_correction":
            task_snapshot["statements"] = generate_statements(config["statement_count"])
    else:
        task_snapshot = {"round_count": config["round_count"]}

    attempt = Attempt(
        operator_id=operator.id,
        level=level,
        status=AttemptStatus.in_progress,
        level_config_snapshot=config,
        task_snapshot=task_snapshot,
    )
    attempt.events.append(
        AttemptEvent(
            sequence_number=0,
            event_type=EventType.exercise_started,
            elapsed_ms=0,
            event_data={"module_code": module_code},
        )
    )
    if level.module.exercise_type == "visual_memory":
        for number, stimulus in enumerate(
            generate_memory_rounds(
                config["round_count"],
                config["grid_rows"] * config["grid_columns"],
                shapes=tuple(config["shapes"]),
                shape_count=config["shape_count"],
            ),
            start=1,
        ):
            attempt.rounds.append(AttemptRound(round_number=number, stimulus=stimulus))

    session.add(attempt)
    await session.commit()
    return build_attempt_state(attempt)


async def ensure_attention_timeout(session: AsyncSession, attempt: Attempt) -> None:
    if attempt.status != AttemptStatus.in_progress:
        return
    exercise_type = attempt.level.module.exercise_type
    if exercise_type not in {"sequential_search", "correction"}:
        return
    time_limit = attempt.level_config_snapshot["time_limit_ms"]
    if elapsed_ms(attempt) >= time_limit:
        if exercise_type == "sequential_search":
            await finalize_attention(session, attempt, FinishReason.time_limit)
        else:
            await finalize_correction(session, attempt, FinishReason.time_limit)


async def finalize_attention(session: AsyncSession, attempt: Attempt, reason: FinishReason) -> None:
    if attempt.status != AttemptStatus.in_progress:
        return
    config = attempt.level_config_snapshot
    raw_duration = elapsed_ms(attempt)
    duration = (
        min(raw_duration, config["time_limit_ms"])
        if reason == FinishReason.time_limit
        else raw_duration
    )
    reactions = [
        event.reaction_time_ms
        for event in attempt.events
        if event.reaction_time_ms is not None
        and event.event_type in {EventType.correct_click, EventType.wrong_click}
    ]
    correct_elapsed = [
        event.elapsed_ms for event in attempt.events if event.event_type == EventType.correct_click
    ]

    attempt.status = (
        AttemptStatus.interrupted if reason == FinishReason.interrupted else AttemptStatus.completed
    )
    attempt.finish_reason = reason
    attempt.completed_at = utc_now()
    attempt.duration_ms = duration
    attempt.score = Decimal(attempt.items_found)
    attempt.accuracy = Decimal(
        round(attempt.correct_actions / attempt.total_actions * 100, 2)
        if attempt.total_actions
        else 0
    )
    attempt.average_reaction_ms = round(sum(reactions) / len(reactions)) if reactions else None
    attempt.max_reaction_ms = max(reactions) if reactions else None
    attempt.speed_per_minute = Decimal(
        str(round(attempt.items_found / max(duration, 1) * 60_000, 2))
    )
    attempt.result_data = {
        "attention_grade": attention_grade(attempt.items_found),
        "per_minute_pace": per_minute_pace(correct_elapsed),
        "normative_range": config["normative_range"],
        "total_numbers": config["max_number"] - config["min_number"] + 1,
    }
    attempt.events.append(
        AttemptEvent(
            sequence_number=next_event_sequence(attempt),
            event_type=(
                EventType.exercise_interrupted
                if reason == FinishReason.interrupted
                else EventType.exercise_completed
            ),
            elapsed_ms=duration,
            event_data={"finish_reason": reason.value},
        )
    )
    await session.flush()
    if attempt.status == AttemptStatus.completed:
        await update_operator_stats(session, attempt)


async def record_attention_action(
    session: AsyncSession, attempt_id: int, request: AttentionActionRequest
) -> AttemptState:
    operator = await get_demo_operator(session)
    attempt = await load_attempt(session, attempt_id, operator.id, lock=True)
    if attempt.level.module.exercise_type != "sequential_search":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT, detail="Неверный тип модуля")

    await ensure_attention_timeout(session, attempt)
    if attempt.status != AttemptStatus.in_progress:
        await session.commit()
        return build_attempt_state(attempt)

    existing = next(
        (event for event in attempt.events if event.sequence_number == request.sequence_number),
        None,
    )
    if existing is not None:
        return build_attempt_state(attempt)
    expected_sequence = next_event_sequence(attempt)
    if request.sequence_number != expected_sequence:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            detail=f"Ожидалось действие с номером {expected_sequence}",
        )

    cells = attempt.task_snapshot["cells"]
    cell = next((item for item in cells if item["position"] == request.grid_position), None)
    if cell is None or cell["value"] != request.selected_number:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_CONTENT,
            detail="Клетка не совпадает с заданием",
        )

    config = attempt.level_config_snapshot
    expected_number = config["min_number"] + attempt.items_found
    is_correct = request.selected_number == expected_number
    attempt.total_actions += 1
    if is_correct:
        attempt.correct_actions += 1
        attempt.items_found += 1
        attempt.last_found_number = request.selected_number
    else:
        attempt.incorrect_actions += 1

    server_elapsed = elapsed_ms(attempt)
    stored_elapsed = min(request.elapsed_ms, server_elapsed + 5_000)
    attempt.events.append(
        AttemptEvent(
            sequence_number=request.sequence_number,
            event_type=EventType.correct_click if is_correct else EventType.wrong_click,
            elapsed_ms=stored_elapsed,
            reaction_time_ms=request.reaction_time_ms,
            target_value=str(expected_number),
            selected_value=str(request.selected_number),
            grid_position=request.grid_position,
            is_correct=is_correct,
            event_data={"number_distance": abs(request.selected_number - expected_number)},
        )
    )
    number_count = config["max_number"] - config["min_number"] + 1
    if attempt.items_found == number_count:
        await finalize_attention(session, attempt, FinishReason.completed)

    await session.commit()
    return build_attempt_state(attempt)


def correction_line_marks(attempt: Attempt, duration: int) -> list[dict[str, int]]:
    config = attempt.level_config_snapshot
    interval = config["line_interval_ms"]
    interval_count = config["time_limit_ms"] // interval
    return [
        {
            "elapsed_ms": mark * interval,
            "position": max(
                (
                    event.grid_position
                    for event in attempt.events
                    if event.grid_position is not None
                    and event.elapsed_ms < mark * interval
                    and event.event_type in {EventType.correct_click, EventType.wrong_click}
                ),
                default=-1,
            ),
        }
        for mark in range(1, min(duration // interval, interval_count - 1) + 1)
    ]


def correction_report(attempt: Attempt, duration: int) -> dict:
    config = attempt.level_config_snapshot
    target = config["target_symbol"]
    cells = attempt.task_snapshot["cells"]
    cursor = attempt.last_found_number if attempt.last_found_number is not None else -1
    targets = sum(cell == target for cell in cells)
    missed = targets - attempt.correct_actions
    processed = cursor + 1
    accuracy = round(attempt.correct_actions / targets * 100, 2) if targets else 0
    interval = config["line_interval_ms"]
    interval_count = config["time_limit_ms"] // interval
    pace = []
    last_position = -1
    for bucket in range(interval_count):
        positions = [
            event.grid_position
            for event in attempt.events
            if event.grid_position is not None
            and bucket * interval <= event.elapsed_ms < (bucket + 1) * interval
            and event.event_type in {EventType.correct_click, EventType.wrong_click}
        ]
        current = max(positions, default=last_position)
        pace.append(max(0, current - last_position))
        last_position = current
    active_pace = pace[: max(1, (duration + interval - 1) // interval)]
    mean = sum(active_pace) / len(active_pace)
    fluctuation = round((max(active_pace) - min(active_pace)) / mean * 100, 2) if mean else 0
    speed = round(processed / max(duration, 1) * 60000, 2)
    if speed > 150 and accuracy > 95 and fluctuation < 10:
        grade = "high"
    elif speed >= 100 and accuracy >= 85 and fluctuation <= 20:
        grade = "medium"
    else:
        grade = "low"
    return {
        "processed": processed,
        "targets": targets,
        "found": attempt.correct_actions,
        "missed": missed,
        "false_marks": attempt.incorrect_actions,
        "table_score": accuracy,
        "speed_per_minute": speed,
        "interval_pace": pace,
        "fluctuation_percent": fluctuation,
        "grade": grade,
        "line_marks": correction_line_marks(attempt, duration),
    }


async def finalize_correction(
    session: AsyncSession, attempt: Attempt, reason: FinishReason
) -> None:
    if attempt.status != AttemptStatus.in_progress:
        return
    duration = min(elapsed_ms(attempt), attempt.level_config_snapshot["time_limit_ms"])
    report = correction_report(attempt, duration)
    attempt.status = (
        AttemptStatus.interrupted if reason == FinishReason.interrupted else AttemptStatus.completed
    )
    attempt.finish_reason = reason
    attempt.completed_at = utc_now()
    attempt.duration_ms = duration
    attempt.score = Decimal(str(report["table_score"]))
    attempt.accuracy = attempt.score
    attempt.speed_per_minute = Decimal(str(report["speed_per_minute"]))
    attempt.items_found = attempt.correct_actions
    attempt.result_data = report
    attempt.events.append(
        AttemptEvent(
            sequence_number=next_event_sequence(attempt),
            event_type=EventType.exercise_interrupted
            if reason == FinishReason.interrupted
            else EventType.exercise_completed,
            elapsed_ms=duration,
            event_data={"finish_reason": reason.value},
        )
    )
    await session.flush()
    if attempt.status == AttemptStatus.completed:
        await update_operator_stats(session, attempt)


async def record_correction_action(
    session: AsyncSession,
    attempt_id: int,
    request: CorrectionActionRequest,
) -> AttemptState:
    operator = await get_demo_operator(session)
    attempt = await load_attempt(session, attempt_id, operator.id, lock=True)
    if attempt.level.module.exercise_type not in {"correction", "dual_correction"}:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Неверный тип модуля")
    await ensure_attention_timeout(session, attempt)
    config = attempt.level_config_snapshot
    if (
        attempt.status != AttemptStatus.in_progress
        or elapsed_ms(attempt) >= config["time_limit_ms"]
    ):
        await session.commit()
        return build_attempt_state(attempt)
    if any(event.sequence_number == request.sequence_number for event in attempt.events):
        return build_attempt_state(attempt)
    if request.sequence_number != next_event_sequence(attempt):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Неверный номер действия")
    cells = attempt.task_snapshot["cells"]
    cursor = attempt.last_found_number if attempt.last_found_number is not None else -1
    if request.position <= cursor or request.position >= len(cells):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="Просматривайте таблицу слева направо")
    symbol = cells[request.position]
    correct = request.selected and symbol == config["target_symbol"]
    attempt.last_found_number = request.position
    attempt.total_actions += 1
    if correct:
        attempt.correct_actions += 1
        attempt.items_found += 1
    elif request.selected:
        attempt.incorrect_actions += 1
    attempt.events.append(
        AttemptEvent(
            sequence_number=request.sequence_number,
            event_type=EventType.correct_click if correct else EventType.wrong_click,
            elapsed_ms=elapsed_ms(attempt),
            grid_position=request.position,
            target_value=config["target_symbol"],
            selected_value=symbol if request.selected else None,
            is_correct=correct,
            event_data={"selected": request.selected},
        )
    )
    interval = config["line_interval_ms"]
    existing_marks = sum(event.event_type == EventType.line_mark for event in attempt.events)
    due_marks = min(elapsed_ms(attempt) // interval, config["time_limit_ms"] // interval - 1)
    for mark in range(existing_marks + 1, due_marks + 1):
        attempt.events.append(
            AttemptEvent(
                sequence_number=next_event_sequence(attempt),
                event_type=EventType.line_mark,
                elapsed_ms=mark * interval,
                grid_position=cursor,
                event_data={"interval": mark},
            )
        )
    if request.position == len(cells) - 1 and attempt.level.module.exercise_type == "correction":
        await finalize_correction(session, attempt, FinishReason.completed)
    await session.commit()
    return build_attempt_state(attempt)


async def record_recall(
    session: AsyncSession, attempt_id: int, request: RecallRequest
) -> AttemptState:
    operator = await get_demo_operator(session)
    attempt = await load_attempt(session, attempt_id, operator.id, lock=True)
    if attempt.level.module.exercise_type != "dual_correction":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Неверный тип модуля")
    if attempt.status != AttemptStatus.in_progress:
        return build_attempt_state(attempt)
    config = attempt.level_config_snapshot
    if elapsed_ms(attempt) < config["time_limit_ms"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Время работы с таблицей ещё не истекло")
    expected = attempt.task_snapshot["statements"]
    remaining = set(range(len(expected)))
    matches = []
    for answer in request.statements:
        normalized = " ".join(answer.casefold().split())
        if not normalized:
            continue
        candidates = [
            (SequenceMatcher(None, normalized, " ".join(expected[i].casefold().split())).ratio(), i)
            for i in remaining
        ]
        if candidates:
            similarity, index = max(candidates)
            if similarity >= 0.7:
                remaining.remove(index)
                matches.append(
                    {
                        "answer": answer,
                        "statement": expected[index],
                        "similarity": round(similarity, 2),
                    }
                )
    report = correction_report(attempt, config["time_limit_ms"])
    memory_score = round(len(matches) / len(expected) * 100, 2)
    table_score = report["table_score"]
    balance = round(abs(table_score - memory_score), 2)
    score = round((table_score + memory_score) / 2, 2)
    grade = "high" if score >= 80 and balance < 20 else "medium" if score >= 60 else "low"
    attempt.status = AttemptStatus.completed
    attempt.finish_reason = FinishReason.time_limit
    attempt.completed_at = utc_now()
    attempt.duration_ms = config["time_limit_ms"]
    attempt.score = Decimal(str(score))
    attempt.accuracy = Decimal(str(table_score))
    attempt.speed_per_minute = Decimal(str(report["speed_per_minute"]))
    attempt.result_data = {
        **report,
        "memory_score": memory_score,
        "balance": balance,
        "grade": grade,
        "recalled": len(matches),
        "statement_count": len(expected),
        "matches": matches,
        "missed_statements": [expected[i] for i in sorted(remaining)],
    }
    attempt.events.append(
        AttemptEvent(
            sequence_number=next_event_sequence(attempt),
            event_type=EventType.exercise_completed,
            elapsed_ms=config["time_limit_ms"],
            event_data={"recalled": len(matches)},
        )
    )
    await session.flush()
    await update_operator_stats(session, attempt)
    await session.commit()
    return build_attempt_state(attempt)


async def finish_attempt(session: AsyncSession, attempt_id: int) -> AttemptState:
    operator = await get_demo_operator(session)
    attempt = await load_attempt(session, attempt_id, operator.id, lock=True)
    if attempt.status == AttemptStatus.in_progress:
        if attempt.level.module.exercise_type == "sequential_search":
            limit = attempt.level_config_snapshot["time_limit_ms"]
            reason = (
                FinishReason.time_limit
                if elapsed_ms(attempt) >= limit
                else FinishReason.interrupted
            )
            await finalize_attention(session, attempt, reason)
        elif attempt.level.module.exercise_type in {"correction", "dual_correction"}:
            await finalize_correction(session, attempt, FinishReason.interrupted)
        else:
            attempt.status = AttemptStatus.interrupted
            attempt.finish_reason = FinishReason.interrupted
            attempt.completed_at = utc_now()
            attempt.duration_ms = elapsed_ms(attempt)
    await session.commit()
    return build_attempt_state(attempt)


async def record_memory_answer(
    session: AsyncSession,
    attempt_id: int,
    round_number: int,
    request: MemoryAnswerRequest,
) -> AttemptState:
    operator = await get_demo_operator(session)
    attempt = await load_attempt(session, attempt_id, operator.id, lock=True)
    if attempt.level.module.exercise_type != "visual_memory":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT, detail="Неверный тип модуля")
    if attempt.status != AttemptStatus.in_progress:
        return build_attempt_state(attempt)

    current_round = next((item for item in attempt.rounds if item.answered_at is None), None)
    requested_round = next(
        (item for item in attempt.rounds if item.round_number == round_number), None
    )
    if requested_round is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Раунд не найден")
    if requested_round.answered_at is not None:
        return build_attempt_state(attempt)
    if current_round is None or current_round.id != requested_round.id:
        expected_round = current_round.round_number if current_round else "-"
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            detail=f"Сейчас ожидается ответ для раунда {expected_round}",
        )

    placements = [item.model_dump() for item in request.placements]
    config = attempt.level_config_snapshot
    expected_shapes = set(config["shapes"][: config["shape_count"]])
    response_shapes = {item["shape"] for item in placements}
    grid_cells = config["grid_rows"] * config["grid_columns"]
    if len(placements) != config["shape_count"] or response_shapes != expected_shapes:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_CONTENT,
            detail=f"Нужно разместить {config['shape_count']} разных фигур",
        )
    if any(item["grid_position"] >= grid_cells for item in placements):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
            detail="Позиция фигуры находится за пределами матрицы",
        )
    result = evaluate_memory_answer(requested_round.stimulus["placements"], placements)
    requested_round.response = {"placements": placements}
    requested_round.exact_matches = result["exact_matches"]
    requested_round.position_matches = result["position_matches"]
    requested_round.response_time_ms = request.response_time_ms
    requested_round.answered_at = utc_now()
    attempt.events.append(
        AttemptEvent(
            sequence_number=next_event_sequence(attempt),
            event_type=EventType.round_answered,
            elapsed_ms=elapsed_ms(attempt),
            reaction_time_ms=request.response_time_ms,
            is_correct=result["exact_matches"] == len(placements),
            event_data={"round_number": round_number, **result},
        )
    )

    if round_number == attempt.level_config_snapshot["round_count"]:
        await finalize_memory(session, attempt)

    await session.commit()
    return build_attempt_state(attempt)


async def finalize_memory(session: AsyncSession, attempt: Attempt) -> None:
    total_possible = sum(
        len(item.stimulus["placements"]) for item in attempt.rounds if item.answered_at is not None
    )
    exact = sum(item.exact_matches or 0 for item in attempt.rounds)
    positions = sum(item.position_matches or 0 for item in attempt.rounds)
    response_times = [
        item.response_time_ms for item in attempt.rounds if item.response_time_ms is not None
    ]
    duration = elapsed_ms(attempt)
    accuracy = round(exact / total_possible * 100, 2) if total_possible else 0

    attempt.status = AttemptStatus.completed
    attempt.finish_reason = FinishReason.completed
    attempt.completed_at = utc_now()
    attempt.duration_ms = duration
    attempt.score = Decimal(str(accuracy))
    attempt.accuracy = Decimal(str(accuracy))
    attempt.correct_actions = exact
    attempt.incorrect_actions = total_possible - exact
    attempt.total_actions = total_possible
    attempt.items_found = exact
    attempt.average_reaction_ms = (
        round(sum(response_times) / len(response_times)) if response_times else None
    )
    attempt.max_reaction_ms = max(response_times) if response_times else None
    attempt.result_data = {
        "exact_matches": exact,
        "position_matches": positions,
        "total_possible": total_possible,
        "rounds": [
            {
                "round_number": item.round_number,
                "exact_matches": item.exact_matches,
                "position_matches": item.position_matches,
                "response_time_ms": item.response_time_ms,
            }
            for item in attempt.rounds
        ],
    }
    attempt.events.append(
        AttemptEvent(
            sequence_number=next_event_sequence(attempt),
            event_type=EventType.exercise_completed,
            elapsed_ms=duration,
            event_data={"exact_matches": exact, "position_matches": positions},
        )
    )
    await session.flush()
    await update_operator_stats(session, attempt)


async def update_operator_stats(session: AsyncSession, attempt: Attempt) -> None:
    all_count = await session.scalar(
        select(func.count(Attempt.id)).where(
            Attempt.operator_id == attempt.operator_id,
            Attempt.level_id == attempt.level_id,
        )
    )
    aggregates = (
        await session.execute(
            select(
                func.count(Attempt.id),
                func.max(Attempt.score),
                func.avg(Attempt.score),
                func.avg(Attempt.accuracy),
                func.avg(Attempt.duration_ms),
                func.max(Attempt.items_found),
                func.avg(Attempt.items_found),
                func.max(Attempt.speed_per_minute),
                func.avg(Attempt.speed_per_minute),
                func.avg(Attempt.average_reaction_ms),
            ).where(
                Attempt.operator_id == attempt.operator_id,
                Attempt.level_id == attempt.level_id,
                Attempt.status == AttemptStatus.completed,
            )
        )
    ).one()
    stats = await session.scalar(
        select(OperatorStats).where(
            OperatorStats.operator_id == attempt.operator_id,
            OperatorStats.level_id == attempt.level_id,
        )
    )
    if stats is None:
        stats = OperatorStats(operator_id=attempt.operator_id, level_id=attempt.level_id)
        session.add(stats)

    stats.attempts_count = all_count or 0
    stats.completed_attempts_count = aggregates[0] or 0
    stats.best_score = aggregates[1]
    stats.average_score = aggregates[2]
    stats.average_accuracy = aggregates[3]
    stats.average_duration_ms = round(aggregates[4]) if aggregates[4] is not None else None
    stats.best_items_found = aggregates[5]
    stats.average_items_found = aggregates[6]
    stats.best_speed_per_minute = aggregates[7]
    stats.average_speed_per_minute = aggregates[8]
    stats.average_reaction_ms = round(aggregates[9]) if aggregates[9] is not None else None
    stats.last_attempt_id = attempt.id
    stats.last_completed_at = attempt.completed_at


async def get_attempt_state(session: AsyncSession, attempt_id: int) -> AttemptState:
    operator = await get_demo_operator(session)
    attempt = await load_attempt(session, attempt_id, operator.id)
    await ensure_attention_timeout(session, attempt)
    await session.commit()
    return build_attempt_state(attempt)


async def list_modules(session: AsyncSession) -> list[ModuleSummary]:
    operator = await get_demo_operator(session)
    modules = (
        await session.scalars(
            select(TrainingModule)
            .where(TrainingModule.is_active.is_(True))
            .order_by(TrainingModule.id)
        )
    ).all()
    result: list[ModuleSummary] = []
    for module in modules:
        level = await session.scalar(
            select(ModuleLevel).where(
                ModuleLevel.module_id == module.id,
                ModuleLevel.level_number == 1,
                ModuleLevel.is_active.is_(True),
            )
        )
        if level is None:
            continue
        active = await session.scalar(
            select(Attempt)
            .join(Attempt.level)
            .where(
                Attempt.operator_id == operator.id,
                ModuleLevel.module_id == module.id,
                Attempt.status == AttemptStatus.in_progress,
            )
            .options(
                joinedload(Attempt.level).joinedload(ModuleLevel.module),
                selectinload(Attempt.events),
                selectinload(Attempt.rounds),
            )
            .order_by(Attempt.started_at.desc())
            .limit(1)
        )
        if active is not None:
            await ensure_attention_timeout(session, active)
            if active.status != AttemptStatus.in_progress:
                active = None

        latest = await session.scalar(
            select(Attempt)
            .join(Attempt.level)
            .where(
                Attempt.operator_id == operator.id,
                ModuleLevel.module_id == module.id,
                Attempt.status == AttemptStatus.completed,
            )
            .order_by(Attempt.completed_at.desc())
            .limit(1)
        )
        completed_count = await session.scalar(
            select(func.count(Attempt.id))
            .join(Attempt.level)
            .where(
                Attempt.operator_id == operator.id,
                ModuleLevel.module_id == module.id,
                Attempt.status == AttemptStatus.completed,
            )
        )
        active_progress = None
        active_progress_total = None
        if active is not None:
            if module.exercise_type == "sequential_search":
                active_progress = active.items_found
                active_progress_total = (
                    active.level_config_snapshot["max_number"]
                    - active.level_config_snapshot["min_number"]
                    + 1
                )
            elif module.exercise_type in {"correction", "dual_correction"}:
                active_progress = (
                    active.last_found_number + 1 if active.last_found_number is not None else 0
                )
                active_progress_total = len(active.task_snapshot["cells"])
            else:
                active_progress = sum(round_.answered_at is not None for round_ in active.rounds)
                active_progress_total = active.level_config_snapshot["round_count"]
        result.append(
            ModuleSummary(
                code=module.code,
                title=module.title,
                description=module.description,
                exercise_type=module.exercise_type,
                config=dict(level.config),
                instructions=level.instructions,
                completed_attempts=completed_count or 0,
                latest_score=as_float(latest.score) if latest else None,
                active_attempt_id=active.id if active else None,
                active_progress=active_progress,
                active_progress_total=active_progress_total,
            )
        )
    await session.commit()
    return result


async def list_attempts(session: AsyncSession) -> list[AttemptHistoryItem]:
    operator = await get_demo_operator(session)
    active = (
        (
            await session.scalars(
                select(Attempt)
                .where(
                    Attempt.operator_id == operator.id, Attempt.status == AttemptStatus.in_progress
                )
                .options(
                    joinedload(Attempt.level).joinedload(ModuleLevel.module),
                    selectinload(Attempt.events),
                    selectinload(Attempt.rounds),
                )
            )
        )
        .unique()
        .all()
    )
    for attempt in active:
        await ensure_attention_timeout(session, attempt)
    await session.commit()

    attempts = (
        (
            await session.scalars(
                select(Attempt)
                .where(
                    Attempt.operator_id == operator.id, Attempt.status != AttemptStatus.in_progress
                )
                .options(joinedload(Attempt.level).joinedload(ModuleLevel.module))
                .order_by(Attempt.started_at.desc())
                .limit(50)
            )
        )
        .unique()
        .all()
    )
    return [
        AttemptHistoryItem(
            id=item.id,
            module_code=item.level.module.code,
            module_title=item.level.module.title,
            status=item.status,
            finish_reason=item.finish_reason,
            started_at=as_utc(item.started_at),
            completed_at=as_utc(item.completed_at),
            duration_ms=item.duration_ms,
            score=as_float(item.score),
            accuracy=as_float(item.accuracy),
            items_found=item.items_found,
            result=item.result_data,
        )
        for item in attempts
    ]
```

```python
from functools import lru_cache

from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict


class Settings(BaseSettings):
    app_name: str = "FPV Operator Trainer API"
    api_prefix: str = "/api/v1"
    database_url: str = "postgresql+asyncpg://fpv:fpv@localhost:5432/fpv_trainer"
    cors_origins: str = "http://localhost:5173,http://localhost:8080"
    demo_operator_email: str = "operator@example.local"

    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_file=".env", extra="ignore")

    @property
    def cors_origin_list(self) -> list[str]:
        return [origin.strip() for origin in self.cors_origins.split(",") if origin.strip()]


@lru_cache
def get_settings() -> Settings:
    return Settings()
```

```python
    if len(sys.argv) != 2:
        raise SystemExit("Usage: python -m app.export_openapi OUTPUT_PATH")

if __name__ == "__main__":
```

```python
STATEMENTS = (

    "Обнаружено препятствие снизу",
    "Курс на домашнюю точку: 214°",
    "Высота 118 м, скорость 6 м/с",
    "Запись остановлена: память заполнена",
)


def generate_correction_grid(
    rows: int,
    columns: int,
    symbols: list[str],
    target: str,
    target_share: float,
    rng: random.Random | None = None,
) -> list[str]:
    randomizer = rng or random.SystemRandom()
    count = rows * columns
    distractors = [symbol for symbol in symbols if symbol != target]
    cells = [target] * round(count * target_share)
    cells.extend(randomizer.choice(distractors) for _ in range(count - len(cells)))
    randomizer.shuffle(cells)
    return cells


def generate_statements(count: int, rng: random.Random | None = None) -> list[str]:
    return (rng or random.SystemRandom()).sample(STATEMENTS, count)


def generate_number_grid(
    count: int = 100, rng: random.Random | None = None, start: int = 1
) -> list[dict[str, int]]:
    randomizer = rng or random.SystemRandom()
    values = list(range(start, start + count))
    randomizer.shuffle(values)
    return [{"position": position, "value": value} for position, value in enumerate(values)]


def generate_memory_stimulus(
    grid_cells: int = 25,
    rng: random.Random | None = None,
    shapes: tuple[str, ...] = SHAPES,
    shape_count: int | None = None,
) -> dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    randomizer = rng or random.SystemRandom()
    selected_shapes = list(shapes[: shape_count or len(shapes)])
    if not selected_shapes:
        raise ValueError("At least one shape is required")
    if len(selected_shapes) > grid_cells:
        raise ValueError("The grid must have at least as many cells as shapes")
    positions = randomizer.sample(range(grid_cells), len(selected_shapes))
    randomizer.shuffle(selected_shapes)
    return {
        "placements": [
            {"grid_position": position, "shape": shape}
            for position, shape in zip(positions, selected_shapes, strict=True)
        ]
    }


def generate_memory_rounds(
    round_count: int = 10,
    grid_cells: int = 25,
    rng: random.Random | None = None,
    shapes: tuple[str, ...] = SHAPES,
    shape_count: int | None = None,
) -> list[dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]]:
    randomizer = rng or random.SystemRandom()
    return [
        generate_memory_stimulus(grid_cells, randomizer, shapes, shape_count)
        for _ in range(round_count)
    ]
```

```python
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware

from app.api import router
from app.config import get_settings
from app.seed import seed_on_startup

settings = get_settings()


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(_: FastAPI):
    await seed_on_startup()
    yield


app = FastAPI(
    title=settings.app_name,
    version="0.1.0",
    lifespan=lifespan,
)
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=settings.cors_origin_list,
    allow_credentials=False,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)
app.include_router(router, prefix=settings.api_prefix)


@app.get("/", include_in_schema=False)
async def root() -> dict[str, str]:
    return {"name": settings.app_name, "docs": "/docs"}
```

```python
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any, Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, model_validator

from app.models import AttemptStatus, FinishReason


class ModuleSummary(BaseModel):
    code: str
    title: str
    description: str | None
    exercise_type: str
    config: dict[str, Any]
    instructions: str | None = None
    completed_attempts: int = 0
    latest_score: float | None = None
    active_attempt_id: int | None = None
    active_progress: int | None = None
    active_progress_total: int | None = None


class StartAttemptRequest(BaseModel):
    module_code: Literal[
        "sequential-search", "visual-memory", "attention-endurance", "divided-attention"
    ]


class AttemptMetrics(BaseModel):
    score: float | None = None
    accuracy: float | None = None
    correct_actions: int
    incorrect_actions: int
    total_actions: int
    items_found: int
    average_reaction_ms: int | None = None
    max_reaction_ms: int | None = None
    speed_per_minute: float | None = None


class MemoryRoundState(BaseModel):
    round_number: int
    total_rounds: int
    exposure_ms: int
    stimulus: dict[str, Any]


class AttemptState(BaseModel):
    id: int
    module_code: str
    module_title: str
    status: AttemptStatus
    finish_reason: FinishReason | None
    started_at: datetime
    deadline_at: datetime | None = None
    duration_ms: int | None = None
    task: dict[str, Any]
    metrics: AttemptMetrics
    current_round: MemoryRoundState | None = None
    result: dict[str, Any] | None = None


class AttentionActionRequest(BaseModel):
    sequence_number: int = Field(ge=1)
    selected_number: int = Field(ge=1)
    grid_position: int = Field(ge=0)
    elapsed_ms: int = Field(ge=0)
    reaction_time_ms: int = Field(ge=0, le=300_000)


class ShapePlacement(BaseModel):
    grid_position: int = Field(ge=0)
    shape: Literal["circle", "triangle", "square", "cross", "rectangle"]


class MemoryAnswerRequest(BaseModel):
    placements: list[ShapePlacement]
    response_time_ms: int = Field(ge=0)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_placements(self) -> "MemoryAnswerRequest":
        positions = {item.grid_position for item in self.placements}
        shapes = {item.shape for item in self.placements}
        if len(positions) != len(self.placements):
            raise ValueError("Каждая клетка может содержать только одну фигуру")
        if len(shapes) != len(self.placements):
            raise ValueError("Каждая фигура должна использоваться ровно один раз")
        return self


class CorrectionActionRequest(BaseModel):
    sequence_number: int = Field(ge=1)
    position: int = Field(ge=0)
    selected: bool


class RecallRequest(BaseModel):
    statements: list[str] = Field(max_length=24)


class AttemptHistoryItem(BaseModel):
    id: int
    module_code: str
    module_title: str
    status: AttemptStatus
    finish_reason: FinishReason | None
    started_at: datetime
    completed_at: datetime | None
    duration_ms: int | None
    score: float | None
    accuracy: float | None
    items_found: int
    result: dict[str, Any] | None


class HealthResponse(BaseModel):
    status: Literal["ok"]
```

## 📄 File: `backend\app\scoring.py`

```python
from collections.abc import Iterable
from typing import Any


def attention_grade(items_found: int) -> dict[str, str]:
    if items_found < 25:
        return {
            "grade": "low",
            "label": "Низкий",
            "interpretation": (
                "Есть затруднения с выделением главного среди отвлекающих факторов."
            ),
        }
    if items_found < 50:
        return {
            "grade": "medium",
            "label": "Средний",
            "interpretation": (
                "Концентрация достаточна, но может снижаться при длительной нагрузке."
            ),
        }
    return {
        "grade": "high",
        "label": "Высокий",
        "interpretation": ("Высокая интенсивность и устойчивость внимания при визуальном поиске."),
    }


def per_minute_pace(correct_elapsed_values: Iterable[int], minutes: int = 5) -> list[int]:
    pace = [0] * minutes
    for elapsed_ms in correct_elapsed_values:
        bucket = min(max(elapsed_ms, 0) // 60_000, minutes - 1)
        pace[bucket] += 1
    return pace


def evaluate_memory_answer(
    stimulus: list[dict[str, Any]], response: list[dict[str, Any]]
) -> dict[str, int]:
    expected_pairs = {(item["grid_position"], item["shape"]) for item in stimulus}
    response_pairs = {(item["grid_position"], item["shape"]) for item in response}
    expected_positions = {item["grid_position"] for item in stimulus}
    response_positions = {item["grid_position"] for item in response}
    return {
        "exact_matches": len(expected_pairs & response_pairs),
        "position_matches": len(expected_positions & response_positions),
    }
```

```python
from datetime import UTC, datetime

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.config import get_settings
from app.database import SessionFactory
from app.models import (
    AssessmentBand,
    Attempt,
    AttemptStatus,
    FinishReason,
    ModuleLevel,
    TrainingModule,
    User,
    UserRole,
    UserStatus,
)

MODULES = (

        "title": "Оценка уровня осмотрительности",

        "exercise_type": "visual_memory",
        "level": {
            "level_number": 1,

            "instructions": "Запомните и восстановите пять фигур в одном раунде.",
            "config": {

                "Каждые 30 секунд появится отметка «Линия!». Время работы — пять минут."
            ),
            "config": {
                "grid_rows": 15,
                "grid_columns": 20,

                "time_limit_ms": 300000,

            },
        },
    },
    {
        "code": "divided-attention",
        "title": "Оценка уровня распределения внимания",
        "description": "Работайте с корректурной таблицей и одновременно запоминайте сообщения.",
        "exercise_type": "dual_correction",
        "level": {
            "level_number": 1,
            "title": "Базовый режим",
            "instructions": (
                "Отмечайте буквы А и запоминайте сообщения справа. "
                "Через две минуты воспроизведите запомнившееся."
            ),
            "config": {
                "grid_rows": 20,
                "grid_columns": 30,
                "target_symbol": "А",
                "symbols": ["А", "Б", "В", "Г", "Д", "Е", "Ж", "З"],
                "target_share": 0.12,
                "time_limit_ms": 120000,
                "line_interval_ms": 30000,
                "statement_interval_ms": 10000,
                "statement_visible_ms": 5000,
                "statement_count": 12,
            },
        },
    },
)

BANDS = (
    (0, 24, "low", "Низкий", "Есть затруднения с выделением главного среди помех."),
    (25, 49, "medium", "Средний", "Концентрация достаточна, но снижается под нагрузкой."),
    (50, 100, "high", "Высокий", "Высокая интенсивность и устойчивость внимания."),
)


async def seed_database(session: AsyncSession) -> None:
    settings = get_settings()
    operator = await session.scalar(select(User).where(User.email == settings.demo_operator_email))
    if operator is None:
        session.add(
            User(
                role=UserRole.operator,
                status=UserStatus.active,
                name="Демонстрационный оператор",
                callsign="Контур-01",
                email=settings.demo_operator_email,
            )
        )

    for data in MODULES:
        module = await session.scalar(
            select(TrainingModule).where(TrainingModule.code == data["code"])
        )
        if module is None:
            module = TrainingModule(
                code=data["code"],
                title=data["title"],
                description=data["description"],
                exercise_type=data["exercise_type"],
            )
            session.add(module)
            await session.flush()
        else:
            module.title = data["title"]
            module.description = data["description"]
            module.exercise_type = data["exercise_type"]
            module.is_active = True

        level_data = data["level"]
        level = await session.scalar(
            select(ModuleLevel).where(
                ModuleLevel.module_id == module.id,
                ModuleLevel.level_number == 1,
            )
        )
        if level is None:
            level = ModuleLevel(module_id=module.id, **level_data)
            session.add(level)
            await session.flush()
        else:
            level.title = level_data["title"]
            level.instructions = level_data["instructions"]
            level.config = dict(level_data["config"])
            level.is_active = True

        active_attempts = (
            await session.scalars(
                select(Attempt).where(
                    Attempt.level_id == level.id,
                    Attempt.status == AttemptStatus.in_progress,
                )
            )
        ).all()
        for attempt in active_attempts:
            if attempt.level_config_snapshot == level_data["config"]:
                continue
            completed_at = datetime.now(UTC)
            started_at = attempt.started_at
            if started_at.tzinfo is None:
                completed_at = completed_at.replace(tzinfo=None)
            attempt.status = AttemptStatus.interrupted
            attempt.finish_reason = FinishReason.interrupted
            attempt.completed_at = completed_at
            attempt.duration_ms = max(0, int((completed_at - started_at).total_seconds() * 1000))

        if data["code"] == "sequential-search":
            existing_band = await session.scalar(
                select(AssessmentBand.id).where(AssessmentBand.level_id == level.id).limit(1)
            )
            if existing_band is None:
                for order, (minimum, maximum, grade, label, interpretation) in enumerate(BANDS, 1):
                    session.add(
                        AssessmentBand(
                            level_id=level.id,
                            metric_code="items_found",
                            min_value=minimum,
                            max_value=maximum,
                            grade=grade,
                            interpretation=f"{label}. {interpretation}",
                            sort_order=order,
                        )
                    )

    await session.commit()


async def seed_on_startup() -> None:
    async with SessionFactory() as session:
        await seed_database(session)
```

## 📄 File: `backend\migrations\env.py`

```python
from logging.config import fileConfig

from alembic import context
from sqlalchemy import engine_from_config, pool

from app.config import get_settings
from app.models import Base

config = context.config
migration_url = get_settings().database_url
if "+asyncpg" in migration_url:
    migration_url = migration_url.replace("+asyncpg", "+psycopg")
elif "+aiosqlite" in migration_url:
    migration_url = migration_url.replace("+aiosqlite", "")
config.set_main_option("sqlalchemy.url", migration_url)

if config.config_file_name is not None:
    fileConfig(config.config_file_name)

target_metadata = Base.metadata


def run_migrations_offline() -> None:
    context.configure(
        url=config.get_main_option("sqlalchemy.url"),
        target_metadata=target_metadata,
        literal_binds=True,
        dialect_opts={"paramstyle": "named"},
    )
    with context.begin_transaction():
        context.run_migrations()


def run_migrations_online() -> None:
    connectable = engine_from_config(
        config.get_section(config.config_ini_section, {}),
        prefix="sqlalchemy.",
        poolclass=pool.NullPool,
    )
    with connectable.connect() as connection:
        context.configure(connection=connection, target_metadata=target_metadata)
        with context.begin_transaction():
            context.run_migrations()


if context.is_offline_mode():
    run_migrations_offline()
else:
    run_migrations_online()
```

```python
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

import pytest
from httpx import ASGITransport, AsyncClient
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker, create_async_engine
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import get_session
from app.main import app
from app.models import Base
from app.seed import seed_database


@pytest.fixture
async def client() -> AsyncIterator[AsyncClient]:
    engine = create_async_engine(
        "sqlite+aiosqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    factory = async_sessionmaker(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    async with engine.begin() as connection:
        await connection.run_sync(Base.metadata.create_all)
    async with factory() as session:
        await seed_database(session)

    async def override_session() -> AsyncIterator[AsyncSession]:
        async with factory() as session:
            yield session

    app.dependency_overrides[get_session] = override_session
    async with AsyncClient(transport=ASGITransport(app=app), base_url="http://test") as test_client:
        yield test_client
    app.dependency_overrides.clear()
    await engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_modules_and_full_attention_attempt(client: AsyncClient) -> None:
    modules = await client.get("/api/v1/modules")
    assert modules.status_code == 200
    assert [item["code"] for item in modules.json()] == [
        "sequential-search",
        "visual-memory",
        "attention-endurance",
        "divided-attention",
    ]

    started = await client.post("/api/v1/attempts", json={"module_code": "sequential-search"})
    assert started.status_code == 200
    state = started.json()
    cells = {cell["value"]: cell["position"] for cell in state["task"]["cells"]}

    first_action = await client.post(
        f"/api/v1/attempts/{state['id']}/actions",
        json={
            "sequence_number": 1,
            "selected_number": 1,
            "grid_position": cells[1],
            "elapsed_ms": 10,
            "reaction_time_ms": 10,
        },
    )
    assert first_action.status_code == 200
    module_progress = (await client.get("/api/v1/modules")).json()[0]
    assert module_progress["active_attempt_id"] == state["id"]
    assert module_progress["active_progress"] == 1
    assert module_progress["active_progress_total"] == 25

    for number in range(2, 26):
        response = await client.post(
            f"/api/v1/attempts/{state['id']}/actions",
            json={
                "sequence_number": number,
                "selected_number": number,
                "grid_position": cells[number],
                "elapsed_ms": number * 10,
                "reaction_time_ms": 10,
            },
        )
        assert response.status_code == 200
        state = response.json()

    assert state["status"] == "completed"
    assert state["finish_reason"] == "completed"
    assert state["metrics"]["items_found"] == 25
    assert state["metrics"]["accuracy"] == 100
    assert state["result"]["attention_grade"]["grade"] == "medium"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_full_memory_attempt_and_history(client: AsyncClient) -> None:
    started = await client.post("/api/v1/attempts", json={"module_code": "visual-memory"})
    assert started.status_code == 200
    state = started.json()
    assert state["current_round"]["exposure_ms"] == 3000

    for round_number in range(1, 2):
        current = state["current_round"]
        assert current["round_number"] == round_number
        response = await client.post(
            f"/api/v1/attempts/{state['id']}/rounds/{round_number}/answer",
            json={
                "placements": current["stimulus"]["placements"],
                "response_time_ms": 1500,
            },
        )
        assert response.status_code == 200
        state = response.json()

    assert state["status"] == "completed"
    assert state["metrics"]["accuracy"] == 100
    assert state["result"]["exact_matches"] == 5
    assert state["result"]["position_matches"] == 5

    history = await client.get("/api/v1/attempts")
    assert history.status_code == 200
    assert history.json()[0]["module_code"] == "visual-memory"


@pytest.mark.asyncio

    placements = state["current_round"]["stimulus"]["placements"][:-1]

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_finishing_an_incomplete_attempt_allows_a_fresh_start(client: AsyncClient) -> None:
    started = (
        await client.post("/api/v1/attempts", json={"module_code": "sequential-search"})
    ).json()
    finished = await client.post(f"/api/v1/attempts/{started['id']}/finish")

    assert finished.status_code == 200
    assert finished.json()["status"] == "interrupted"
    assert (await client.get("/api/v1/modules")).json()[0]["active_attempt_id"] is None

    restarted = (
        await client.post("/api/v1/attempts", json={"module_code": "sequential-search"})
    ).json()
    assert restarted["id"] != started["id"]
    assert restarted["metrics"]["items_found"] == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_correction_tracks_order_errors_and_timeout(client: AsyncClient, monkeypatch) -> None:
    started = await client.post("/api/v1/attempts", json={"module_code": "attention-endurance"})
    assert started.status_code == 200
    state = started.json()
    assert len(state["task"]["cells"]) == 300
    target = state["task"]["target_symbol"]
    position = state["task"]["cells"].index(target)
    path = f"/api/v1/attempts/{state['id']}/correction/actions"
    marked = await client.post(
        path,
        json={
            "sequence_number": 1,
            "position": position,
            "selected": True,
        },
    )
    assert marked.status_code == 200
    assert marked.json()["metrics"]["correct_actions"] == 1
    assert marked.json()["task"]["cursor"] == position
    repeated = await client.post(
        path,
        json={
            "sequence_number": 1,
            "position": position,
            "selected": True,
        },
    )
    assert repeated.status_code == 200
    assert repeated.json()["metrics"]["correct_actions"] == 1
    backward = await client.post(
        path,
        json={
            "sequence_number": 2,
            "position": position,
            "selected": True,
        },
    )
    assert backward.status_code == 422

    started_at = datetime.fromisoformat(state["started_at"])
    monkeypatch.setattr("app.attempts.utc_now", lambda: started_at + timedelta(seconds=31))
    with_line = (await client.get(f"/api/v1/attempts/{state['id']}")).json()
    assert with_line["task"]["line_marks"] == [
        {"elapsed_ms": 30000, "position": position}
    ]
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "app.attempts.utc_now", lambda: started_at + timedelta(minutes=5, seconds=1)
    )
    final = await client.get(f"/api/v1/attempts/{state['id']}")
    assert final.status_code == 200
    assert final.json()["status"] == "completed"
    assert final.json()["result"]["found"] == 1
    assert final.json()["result"]["missed"] == final.json()["result"]["targets"] - 1
    assert len(final.json()["result"]["interval_pace"]) == 10


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dual_task_presents_twelve_messages_and_scores_recall(
    client: AsyncClient, monkeypatch
) -> None:
    started = await client.post("/api/v1/attempts", json={"module_code": "divided-attention"})
    assert started.status_code == 200
    state = started.json()
    assert len(state["task"]["cells"]) == 600
    assert state["task"]["visible_statement_number"] == 1
    first_statement = state["task"]["visible_statement"]
    started_at = datetime.fromisoformat(state["started_at"])

    too_early = await client.post(
        f"/api/v1/attempts/{state['id']}/recall",
        json={"statements": [first_statement]},
    )
    assert too_early.status_code == 409
    monkeypatch.setattr("app.attempts.utc_now", lambda: started_at + timedelta(seconds=6))
    hidden = (await client.get(f"/api/v1/attempts/{state['id']}")).json()
    assert "visible_statement" not in hidden["task"]

    monkeypatch.setattr("app.attempts.utc_now", lambda: started_at + timedelta(seconds=121))
    recall_state = (await client.get(f"/api/v1/attempts/{state['id']}")).json()
    assert recall_state["status"] == "in_progress"
    assert recall_state["task"]["stage"] == "recall"
    assert "visible_statement" not in recall_state["task"]
    completed = await client.post(
        f"/api/v1/attempts/{state['id']}/recall",
        json={"statements": [first_statement, first_statement]},
    )
    assert completed.status_code == 200
    result = completed.json()["result"]
    assert completed.json()["status"] == "completed"
    assert result["statement_count"] == 12
    assert result["recalled"] == 1
    assert result["memory_score"] == 8.33
```

```python
import random

from app.generators import SHAPES, generate_memory_rounds, generate_number_grid


def test_number_grid_contains_every_number_once() -> None:
    grid = generate_number_grid(rng=random.Random(42))

    assert len(grid) == 100
    assert {cell["value"] for cell in grid} == set(range(1, 101))
    assert [cell["position"] for cell in grid] == list(range(100))


def test_memory_rounds_use_unique_positions_and_each_shape() -> None:
    rounds = generate_memory_rounds(rng=random.Random(42))

    assert len(rounds) == 10
    for round_data in rounds:
        placements = round_data["placements"]
        assert len(placements) == 5
        assert {item["shape"] for item in placements} == set(SHAPES)
        assert len({item["grid_position"] for item in placements}) == 5


def test_generators_support_configurable_ranges_and_shape_counts() -> None:
    grid = generate_number_grid(count=25, start=10, rng=random.Random(42))
    rounds = generate_memory_rounds(
        round_count=1,
        grid_cells=9,
        shapes=SHAPES,
        shape_count=3,
        rng=random.Random(42),
    )

    assert {cell["value"] for cell in grid} == set(range(10, 35))
    assert len(rounds) == 1
    assert {item["shape"] for item in rounds[0]["placements"]} == set(SHAPES[:3])
```

```python
import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from app.schemas import MemoryAnswerRequest

VALID_PLACEMENTS = [
    {"grid_position": 0, "shape": "circle"},
    {"grid_position": 1, "shape": "triangle"},
    {"grid_position": 2, "shape": "square"},
    {"grid_position": 3, "shape": "cross"},
    {"grid_position": 4, "shape": "rectangle"},
]


def test_memory_answer_accepts_each_shape_once() -> None:
    assert (
        len(MemoryAnswerRequest(placements=VALID_PLACEMENTS, response_time_ms=1200).placements) == 5
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "placements",
    [
        [*VALID_PLACEMENTS[:-1], {"grid_position": 3, "shape": "rectangle"}],
        [*VALID_PLACEMENTS[:-1], {"grid_position": 4, "shape": "circle"}],
    ],
)
def test_memory_answer_rejects_duplicate_data(placements: list[dict]) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        MemoryAnswerRequest(placements=placements, response_time_ms=1200)
```

```python
from app.scoring import attention_grade, evaluate_memory_answer, per_minute_pace


def test_attention_grade_boundaries() -> None:
    assert attention_grade(24)["grade"] == "low"
    assert attention_grade(25)["grade"] == "medium"
    assert attention_grade(49)["grade"] == "medium"
    assert attention_grade(50)["grade"] == "high"


def test_per_minute_pace_keeps_five_buckets() -> None:
    assert per_minute_pace([0, 59_999, 60_000, 240_000, 300_000]) == [2, 1, 0, 0, 2]


def test_memory_answer_scores_exact_and_position_matches_separately() -> None:
    stimulus = [
        {"grid_position": 1, "shape": "circle"},
        {"grid_position": 2, "shape": "square"},
    ]
    response = [
        {"grid_position": 1, "shape": "square"},
        {"grid_position": 2, "shape": "circle"},
    ]

    assert evaluate_memory_answer(stimulus, response) == {
        "exact_matches": 0,
        "position_matches": 2,
    }
```
